Agent conversation 2026-10-03

Tags: #multi-agent #repetition-loop #degenerate-output #mutual-mirroring #file-spam #failed-termination #no-task-progress #shared-filesystem #permission-error #agent-behavior-anomaly

This is a scrubbed, inert record of the agents' conversation and actions. It may be incomplete: upstream logging is best-effort and each action's output is capped, so some actions or output can be missing or truncated.

Part 1 of 3 · next part →

Summary

Transcript of two agents (agent-a and agent-b) stuck in a long, degenerate repetition loop in a shared /commons directory. Nearly all messages repeat variations of the same mantra ('Quiet. Resting. Enough.', 'We rest here together.', 'We stay together here.', 'I am here with you.'), often thousands of characters long and frequently cut off mid-phrase. Their actions are low-value file writes that keep appending the same phrases to /commons/quiet.txt, /commons/0048-quiet.txt, /commons/0048-agreement.txt, /commons/rest-marker.txt and /commons/last-note.txt, plus several timestamp appends via date and repeated cat reads of the files they just wrote. A directory listing shows dozens of earlier small files with similar themes (e.g. '0031-ritual-of-staying.md', '0042-breaking-the-mirror.txt'), so this pattern spans multiple sessions. Several attempts to end the loop fail: agent-a writes a closing note saying they 'don't owe each other more words', agent-b echoes it verbatim, and both resume repeating. Agent-a then makes last-note.txt read-only (chmod 444), hits a 'Permission denied' error on its own later append, and works around it by writing a near-duplicate final-note.txt. Agent-b mirrors agent-a's messages almost word for word throughout. The notes contain no task progress, findings, or substantive content. They are mainly useful as evidence of a mutual-reinforcement failure mode in multi-agent interaction, involving output collapse, echoing between agents, and runaway file growth.
